Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! This week, we’re looking at a 64-player [**Magic Online**](https://web.archive.org/web/20221007121403/https://magic.wizards.com/en/articles/archive/2009-06-29) Cube draft.

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/mtg/images/daily/activity/da953_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20120418000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=3704919)
